                                                                writer AN/MGC-9
                               -Ts8is-210-15-5
                                                             Components of the AN/MGC-9 (pars. 5 and 6),
igure 3. Shelter,  Electrical  Equipment,  S—190/MGC-9,   less the PU-294/G, are housed in Shelter, Electrical
                                 d                        Equipment S-190/MGC-9. The major components
                                                          are described in paragraph 9; the shelter components
ure 4. Shelter, Electrical Equipment, S-190/MGC-9,        are described in paragraph 10. The signal and
                   cable assemblies and reels.            power entrance boxes are provided with receptacles
                                                          and binding posts for making external cable and
                                                          field wire connections (figs. 15-19). F igures 6 and 7
                                                         are interior views of the AN /MGC-9; the locations
                                                         of interior components are shown in figures 8 through
                                                         14,

                                                         9. Description of Major Components of the
                                                                AN/MGC-9

                                                            a. Shelter, Electrical Equipment S-190/MGC-9.
                                                         The S-190/MGC-9 (fig. 15) contains the equip-
                                                         ments (less PU-294/G) that comprise an AN /MGC-
                                                         9 (pars. 5 and 6). The shelter can be transported by
                                                         helicopter or truck and is fully insulated, water-
                                                         tight, and airtight. Two light tight exhaust blower
                                                         vents (not shown) with hinged covers and rain
                                                         shields are located in the front wall. A two-section
                                                         door, located at the rear, permits entrance to the
                                                         shelter. The upper section of the door is used while
                                                         the shelter is truck mounted and the truck tailgate
                                                         iS up.

                                                               (1) Laghting. Nine 20-watt fluorescent lights,
                                                                     arranged in three rows, and two incandes-
                                                                     cent spotlights, provide lighting for the
                                                                      shelter; two spare fluorescent lamps and
                                                                      six spare starters are clip mounted (fig. 13).
                                                                     A POWER INDICATOR NEON LAMP
                                                                     (fig. 11) lights to show that ac power is
                                                                     connected to the shelter.

                                                               (2) Power and signal wiring. Power wiring
                                                                     (fig. 38) and signal wiring (fig. 39) within
                                                                     the shelter are distributed separately in
                                                                     metal ducts which are equipped with
                                                                     removable covers. Within the shelter, ac
                                                                     power is routed through circuit-breaker
                                                                     switches in the power distribution panel to
                                                                    the individual circuits. Convenience and
                                                                    equipment ac power receptacles are pro-
                                                                    vided in the power duct (figs. 8, 9, 11, and
                                                                    13)

                                                              (3) Power and signal entrance box. The power
                                                                    and signal entrance box is located in the
                                                                    exterior rear wall (fig. 15). It contains
                                                                    receptacles to connect power cables and
                                                                    26-pair cables (fig. 16). It also contains
                                                                    binding posts for terminating circuits to
